Tested bit-exact over 8k cases (4..64 verts per hull — the full CONVEX_MAX_VERTICES_POLYGONS range, warm-start sizes 0..4 with garbage in the unused packed bytes to prove the masking, exact-zero aToB.p fixtures for the fallback): vertices/cached vectors to tolerance, all packed indices/sizes exact, unused cache slots verified zero; full ctest 433/433; 12-mutation battery: 11 caught (vertex2Shape rotate order, missing +p, wrong scale, unpack shift/mask, subtraction order, encode byte order, b() extraction, re-pack shift, initialDir gate, seed-loop bound, wsA/wsB swap), 1 equivalent survivor — the a() extraction 0xFF→0x7F, unobservable because hull vertex indices are asserted &lt; CONVEX_MAX_VERTICES_POLYGONS=64 so bit 7 of a packed index byte is structurally 0.  The collide() post-GJK decision + sphere-path store (tranche 149, cudaGJKEPA.cu L1354-1445) finishes collide()'s own logic: with GJK/EPA results injected (the drivers are separately verified shaders), it replicates the eDISTANT early-out, the anomaly test (eCLOSE && closestPointDir·direction &lt; 0.999, dot associating (x+y)+z), the EPA gate (!separated || anomaly), the degenerate re-run (cache reset + second GJK/EPA, separated re-derived from the second EPA), and the type0==eSPHERE witness store (per-component products summed in the CUDA shuffle order (x+y)+z; slot 0 = pointA 0 (sphere centre), pointB witnessB, normalPen (−directionFinal, sep); numManifoldContacts=1).  The adversarial review caught a genuine modeling gap in the first cut — CUDA reads gjkOutput.direction at two program points with different values (the anomaly gate sees the FIRST GJK's direction, but epa() overwrites output.direction so the sphere store sees the post-EPA one) and the shader had conflated them into one input — fixed by splitting the input into direction (anomaly gate) and directionFinal (store), with fixtures feeding distinct vectors whenever the EPA gate can fire.  Tested exact over 12k cases (all gjk/epa result combinations, degenerate every 4th, exact-aligned closestPointDir fixtures driving the no-EPA path, 0.999 boundary margin-skipped): flags exact, sphere store to tolerance; full ctest 435/435; 13-mutation battery: 12 caught (early-out selector, anomaly comparison flip, OR→AND gate, both separated derivations, retry gate flip, epaResult2→1, sep sum/order/sign, store normal sign, wB→wA, and the directionFinal→direction conflation itself), 1 provably-equivalent survivor (dropping the eCLOSE conjunct from anomaly — anomaly is only read inside !separated||anomaly where !separated already covers every non-eCLOSE case, so the conjunct is redundant by boolean algebra).  prepareLostFoundPairs Stage1+Stage2 (tranche 150, cudaGJKEPA.cu L1468-1558) is the lost/found touch-report compaction: Stage1 runs the scanKernel1of2 per-block exclusive scan over the 2·numPairs flag values (first half lost-touch, second half found-touch) into tempRunsum + per-block totals, Stage2 adds the cross-block prefix and applies CompactContactManagersFunctor — for every flagged index the manager's {nbPatches, prevPatches, statusFlag} (nbContacts deliberately NOT copied, it is commented out in the CUDA) and its opaque contact-manager pointer are written at the scan offset, the exclusive prefix at idx==numPairs (= the lost count) goes to lostAndTotalReportedPairsCount.x and the grand total to .y — with the manager pointers modelled as opaque 32-bit handles (the kernel only copies them) and the compacted output using the real PxsContactManagerOutputCounts little-endian byte layout.  Tested exact (all-integer) over 8 size configurations (1, 5, 128, 300, 4096, 6000, 9000, 7777 pairs — block-boundary, multi-iteration, sparse 2% and dense 97% flag densities), comparing every compacted slot + both counters with untouched slots verified against a sentinel; the adversarial review caught a launch-config divergence in the first cut (the shader scanned with 256-thread blocks while CUDA launches COMPACT_LOST_FOUND_PAIRS with 512-thread blocks — final outputs are partition-independent but the tempRunsum/blockAccumulation intermediate buffers diverge bit-wise), fixed to BLOCK=512/16 warps with the test now validating BOTH intermediate buffers against the 512-chunked reference; full ctest 437/437; 12-mutation battery fully killed (inclusive→exclusive scan, missing block prefix ×2, scan length halved, flag test inverted, idx%numPairs dropped, Counts byte-order swap, statusFlag source byte, lost-boundary off-by-one, total off-by-one, block-total accumulation, handle index).  writeContactsToStream4threads (tranche 151, cudaGJKEPA.cu L1622-1875) is the contact-stream writer that runs after collide(): one warp iteration = 8 pairs × 4 lanes, per pair the shape/flip setup (flip=(type1&lt;type0) swaps types/scales/cacheRefs but NOT material indices, radius=scale0.y post-flip for sphere/capsule, contactDist=cd[ref0]+cd[ref1]), the manifold mNbContacts 0x80000000-invalidation-bit clear-in-memory (count treated 0 when set), the group average normal ((n0+n2)+(n1+n3) butterfly, normalized when magSq≥1e-5 else the storing lane's own normal, t1-rotated, flip-negated), world pointB with sep=normPen.w−radius and the keep ballot (sep≤contactDist), the average-point extra ballot, the warp-lane-0 byte reservation (16/4 bytes per contact, overflow ≥limit → error flag + 0xFFFFFFFF with force checked only when contacts fit), per-lane popc compaction, the group average point (summed over lane INDICES below the RE-derived kept count — CUDA's tidInGroup test, an unkept low lane can contribute while a kept high lane does not, ported exactly) written first, kept points after, and the tid0 epilogue (offsets 0xFFFFFFFF when empty, the force offset left to wrap in uint arithmetic when only the force reservation failed, nbContacts+1 for the average, unswapped materials).  Tested vs an exact CPU transcription over 6k iterations (random types driving flips, sphere/capsule radius paths, invalidation bits, invalid lanes, contact/force/both-tight overflow fixtures, insertAveragePoint alternating, and a crafted two-opposing-normals cancellation fixture that reaches the magSq&lt;1e-5 fallback): stream slots + avgNorm to tolerance, counters/flags/offsets/params exact, keep and 1e-5 boundaries margin-skipped; the adversarial review then caught three bit-exactness divergences in the averaging arithmetic — the avgPt sum was sequential where CUDA's shfl_xor butterfly associates (p0+p2)+(p1+p3), and both the avgNorm normalization and the avgPt/count division used true division where PxVec3::operator/= and cutil's operator/=(float4,float) are reciprocal-then-multiply — all three fixed (shader + reference); full ctest 439/439; 18-mutation battery: 16 caught, 2 boundary equivalents (magSq≥→&gt; at exactly 1e-5 and keep ≤→&lt; at exactly sep==contactDist — both measure-zero and excluded by the margin skip).  The finishContactsKernel tail + writePatchesToStream4threads (tranche 152, cudaGJKEPA.cu L2052-2167 + L1895-2007) completes the convex-convex output pipeline: scratch startIdx/internalFlags zeroing, the allflags status update (oldStatus=byte 2, prevPatches=byte 1; clear eTOUCH_KNOWN, set eHAS_TOUCH/eHAS_NO_TOUCH by nbContacts; rewrite ((prevPatches&lt;&lt;8|status)&lt;&lt;16)|(numPatches&lt;&lt;8)), touchChange=(prevTouch^curTouch)||!prevKnown and patchChange=(prevPatches!=numPatches), the lane-31 patch reservation (64 B/patch, overflow STRICTLY &gt; the limit — unlike the contact stream's ≥ — setting PATCH_BUFFER_OVERFLOW=4), the overflow rollback (mask=0, scratch nb=0, HAS_NO_TOUCH rewrite, touchChange=prevTouch, patchChange=prevPatches≠0), contactPatches=patchIndex+64·popcBelow written for every valid lane when the reservation held, combineMaterials (the tranche-43 core) with the u8 materialFlags cast, and the 4-uint4 PxContactPatch payload assembled via the patchOffsets table (1.0f invMass/invInertia constants; norm+restitution; frictions+damping+contactUnion(startIdx|nb&lt;&lt;16|matFlags&lt;&lt;24); materialUnion(0|mat0&lt;&lt;16)+materialUnion2(mat1)+two 0.0f).  Tested exact over 8k warps (random status words, tight-limit overflow, exact-fit fixtures pinning the strict-&gt;, invalid lanes, empty warps, compliant/friction-disabled/high-bit-flag materials exercising the u8 cast); full ctest 441/441; 18-mutation battery: 17 caught, 1 provably-equivalent survivor (the rollback's scratch-nbContacts store is dead within the kernel — its only consumer, the patch payload, is gated by the patchMask the rollback just zeroed, and the shared scratch dies at kernel end).  convexConvexNphase_stage1Kernel (tranche 153, cudaGJKEPA.cu L2170-2388) is the per-pair persistent-manifold refresh + rerun decision before collide(): shape/flip setup, PCM margins (sphere/capsule margin=scale.y with zero extents, else min(minExtent·0.25, toleranceLength·0.05)), aToB=t1.transformInv(t0), the manifold refresh (localAInB=aToB.transform(pointA), pen re-derived as (localAInB−pointB)·normal, keep iff projected 2d distance² ≤ (0.8·minMargin)², in-place compaction with stale trailing slots), and bRunNarrowphase = count-changed else invalidate_BoxConvex (per-count ratio/quat thresholds from the 3-entry sphere / 5-entry convex tables, max-component deltaP, quat dots vs the previous frame, acos travel distances with radiusA=0 on the sphere path), with the counts word newNb|(run?initNb&lt;&lt;8), tempKeepPairArray, and the moreData rewrite ((aToB.p,0), t0.q, t1.q) on rerun.  Tested over 12k pairs (flip fixtures, sphere/capsule + convex paths, pointA constructed through aToB⁻¹ so keeps and the invalidate path are dense, small-angle perturbations of EXACTLY ONE previous quat sweeping the quat-threshold and travel-distance bands — with radiusA=0 on the sphere path these pin each side of both || gates): counts/flags exact, manifold/moreData floats to tolerance, all boundaries margin-skipped; sphere/capsule fixtures cap contacts at 2 (beyond that the CUDA would index past its 3-entry threshold tables); full ctest 443/443; 18-mutation battery: 15 caught, 3 provably-equivalent survivors (keep ≤→&lt; at the exact boundary — measure-zero, margin-skipped; bRun ≠→&lt; — kept contacts are a subset so newNb ≤ initialNb structurally; dropping the acos deltaQ&lt;1 guard — acos(≥1) is NaN or 0 and both fail the strict &gt; identically).  convexConvexNphase_stage2Kernel_core's setup/teardown glue (tranche 154, cudaGJKEPA.cu L2390-2641; the collide() call itself is the previously-verified gjk/epa/contact-gen shader stack, its outputs injected) closes the convex-convex pipeline: whole-shape flip, the synthetic sphere/capsule vertices ((0,0,0,0) / (±1,0,0,0)) with expandedMargin = convexMargin0 = scale.x for the sphere (the CUDA reads .x here, unlike the .y used elsewhere — ported verbatim) and scale.y for the capsule, blob-header count decode, inSphereRadius = extents.w·minScale with the CUDA quirk that BOTH minScale gates test shape1.type==eBOX (L2542/L2569), contactDistance = cd0+cd1+expandedMargin, replaceBreakingThreshold = fminf(margin0,margin1)·0.05, the stage-1 counts-word unpack, and the mNbContacts writeback numManifoldContacts | (bIntersection ? 0 : 0x80000000).  Tested exact over 10k pairs (all four geometry kinds on both sides, flips, no-intersection writebacks); the adversarial review confirmed the arithmetic exact and prompted two hardening follow-ups — the flip-sensitive scratch state (rot0/rot1 mesh-scale rotations, material indices, tB) is now modelled, swapped and verified with two extra swap mutants (both caught), and the writeback comment now states the real CUDA mechanism (the memory store covers words 9..12; runGJK's warm-start updates live in lane registers stage2 never stores, so memory keeps stage1's words 13..15); full ctest 445/445; 14-mutation battery: 13 caught, 1 provably-equivalent survivor (refreshed-count &amp;0xFF→&amp;0xF — stage1 writes newNb ≤ 4 into that byte, so bit 4+ is structurally 0).  With this, every kernel of cudaGJKEPA.cu except the convex-plane path (convexPlaneNphase_kernel_core) is ported.  The convex-vs-plane path opened with getPolygonIndexFromPlaneNormal (tranche 155a, cudaGJKEPA.cu L2672-2773): the face selector that warp-argmaxes the covariant-transformed (shape2Vertex: rotateInv(rotate(n)/scale), then getNormalized) hull polygon normals against the plane normal — per-lane strict-&lt; bests (first max in a lane wins ties), cross-lane lowest-winning-lane tie-break — plus the edge pass (per-edge averaged loadNormal face normals, strict-&gt; per-lane bests, warp max + the 5e-4 edge-preference bias) and the edge-path face pick (dp0&gt;dp1 chooses the edge's better face, the other becomes plane2).  Tested exact over 12k hulls (4..64 polygons, 0..96 edges including the zero-edge face path, anisotropic scale+rotation, and crafted identity-transform fixtures with bitwise-duplicated planes producing EXACT per-lane (0 vs 32), cross-lane (2 vs 17 — also distinguishing a halved lane mask) and duplicate-edge ties — exact ties are deterministic on both sides and compared, only genuine near-ties are margin-skipped); full ctest 447/447; 12-mutation battery: 10 caught (covariant divide, per-lane/cross-lane/edge tie-breaks via the crafted exact ties, bias sign, edge-face pick, byte extraction, edge-average sign, lane-mask halving, abs-projection), 2 provably-equivalent survivors (the bias gate &gt;→≥ — differs only at exact bestEdge==bestProjection equality, measure-zero and margin-skipped since the +5e-4 sum is not platform-stable; and the getNormalized m&gt;0→≥ guard — divergence needs an edge whose two face normals sum to exactly zero, impossible for adjacent convex-hull faces, and the resulting NaN loses every strict-&gt; comparison exactly like a non-winner).  The convex-plane manifold refresh + rerun decision (tranche 155b, cudaGJKEPA.cu L2856-2950 + L3061-3070) differs from the convex-convex stage1 refresh in load-bearing ways ported verbatim: bToA = planeTransform.transformInv(convexTransform) with the working normal n = bToA.rotateInv((1,0,0)).getNormalized(); the refresh brings the PLANE-space pointA back with bToA.transformInv and uses v = localBInB − localAInB (REVERSED); NO compaction (only the ballot count matters, and in the no-rerun case the ballot is structurally dense); invalidate_BoxConvex runs with −bToA.p, radiusA = radiusB = |extents·scale| and the ConvexPlane quat table (five 0.99996 entries) but the CONVEX position-ratio table; the no-rerun reload emits p = bToA.transform(pointB) and sep = the lane's refreshed dist with mask = (1&lt;&lt;nbContacts)−1.  Tested over 12k pairs (pointA constructed through bToA with controlled in-plane offsets driving keeps and drops, seeded previous-frame data, small-angle single-quat perturbations sweeping the very tight 0.99996 band): counts/flags/mask exact, frame + reload floats to tolerance, all boundaries margin-skipped; full ctest 449/449; the adversarial review then caught a genuine fidelity bug — radiusB fed the invalidate travel-distance test with the SCALED extents while the CUDA (L2946) uses the RAW blob extents (only calculatePCMConvexMargin scales internally; the plane kernel, unlike stage1, never pre-scales) — fixed in shader and reference with a new raw-vs-scaled mutant verified caught; 13-mutation battery: 12 caught (quat-multiply order, rotate-vs-rotateInv normal, the REVERSED v, transformInv-vs-transform on pointA, forced-false rerun gate, ConvexPlane-vs-convex quat threshold, −bToA.p sign, zeroed travel radius, reload transform direction, reload sep, tolerance margin factor, scaled-radius regression), 1 boundary-equivalent survivor (keep ≤→&lt; at exact d²==threshold² equality — measure-zero, margin-skipped).  The 155a review follow-up also hardened the planePolyIndex test: the near-tie margin skip now fires in EITHER direction (a challenger winning by 0&lt;Δ&lt;TIE previously went unflagged — a flakiness risk under non-correctly-rounded Vulkan arithmetic), and the per-lane face-tie fixture now duplicates the WINNING plane into its own lane (34&amp;31==2), making the first-max-wins tie-break genuinely observable.  The convex-plane contact generation (tranche 155c, cudaGJKEPA.cu L2952-3059) inlines the 155a selector (called with −n), then runs the vertex-emission loop whose polygon-descriptor read is LOOP-INVARIANT (vRef8NbVertsMinIndex[bestPlaneBIdx] at cu L2966 — the plane2 iteration re-emits the FIRST face's vertices and the reduce trims the duplicates; ported verbatim), the per-vertex pipeline (vertexData8[getVRef8+lane] → vertex2Shape (multiply) → bToA.transform, signDist = plane-space x, ballot &lt; contactDist), the &gt;16-survivor SPARSE-mask reduce with the uniform (1,0,0) normal, shared compaction at totalContacts+popc-below, the post-loop dense &gt;4 reduce, and the manifold write (B = bToA.transformInv(p), A = p with x −= sep, NP = (n, sep)) plus the moreData/mNbContacts writes — all skipped entirely at zero contacts.  Tested over 8k synthesized hulls (real descriptor/vertex-byte tables including vRef8 &gt; 255 fixtures pinning the full-PxU16 extraction, faces up to 20 verts driving the &gt;16 reduce, swept plane offsets driving zero/partial/full emission, the edge path): counts/masks/indices exact, floats to tolerance, all boundaries margin-skipped; full ctest 451/451; 16-mutation battery: 14 caught (selector sign, the loop-invariant descriptor quirk itself, divide-vs-multiply vertex transform, reduce trigger threshold, descriptor field extractions, transformInv direction, pOnPlane sign, moreData sign, reduce argmin/re-pick flips, inactive-lane sentinel, count accumulation, second-iteration removal), 2 provably-equivalent survivors (ballot &lt;→≤ at exact signDist==contactDist — measure-zero, margin-skipped; and the dense reduce &gt;4→≥4 — with exactly four candidates the four picks come from disjoint remaining masks, so all four bits are always selected and the mask is unchanged).  `npCvxPlaneOut.slang` finishes the convex-plane kernel — and with it the last unported block of cudaGJKEPA.cu — with the output tail: the contact/force reservations (16·nb / 4·nb bytes) whose overflow tests add only ONE element size ((offset+16) &gt; limit, (offset+4) &gt; limit) and are independent ifs, so both overflow flags can set in the same pass; the world-space contact-stream write (planeTransform.transform(p), sep) at warpScanExclusive slots, with the force output pointer written verbatim even when it is the wrapped 0xFFFFFFFF of a force-only overflow and BOTH output pointers NULL on contact overflow; the finish-style allflags/touch/patch-change rewrite; the single-patch reservation ((patchIndex+64) &gt; limit) with its rollback INSIDE the lane-0 gate (no ballot: HAS_NO_TOUCH + numPatches=0 rewrite, nbContacts=0, touchChange=previouslyHadTouch, patchChange=prevPatches≠0); and the insertIntoPatchStream call arguments (patchIndex, world normal planeTransform.rotate((1,0,0)) — localNormal is never reassigned in the plane kernel — and nbContacts; the stream write itself is the tranche-45 shader).  Tested over 8k fixtures (all three overflow paths, the both-overflow independence, exact-fit strict-&gt; boundaries, wrapped force offsets, sparse reduce masks, empty manifolds): integers exact, rotated floats to tight tolerance; full ctest 453/453; 20-mutation battery: 20 caught (both strict-&gt; boundaries, the one-element-vs-full-reservation quirk, else-if coupling of the independent overflow tests, wrapped-offset nulling, scan off-by-one, dropped translation, status/prevPatches/allflags encodings, patch reservation size, all rollback fields, patch normal axis, and the 64-bit overflow-compare promotions: CUDA's offset + sizeof(...) is size_t arithmetic, so counters within one element of 2^32 overflow instead of wrapping — flagged by the adversarial review (its only finding beyond hygiene; everything else confirmed faithful, incl. an independent 16-mutant distinguishability probe), ported with explicit uint64_t and pinned by high-region fixtures).  The 155c adversarial review also surfaced a real bit-fidelity hole, fixed forward in the planeGen shader: at totalContacts == 32 (reachable — the edge path re-emits a 16-verts-in-contact face) PTX's clamped shift makes CUDA compute (1 &lt;&lt; 32) − 1 = 0xFFFFFFFF while SPIR-V wraps the shift mod 32, so both shader and C++ reference now clamp explicitly — pinned by rigged fixtures forcing the 16+16 duplication plus a regression mutant, with the sparse-reduce coverage counter fixed to count where the reduce actually runs and the moreData quat compare widened to all four words.  `npCvxCorrelate.slang` opens the post-cudaGJKEPA phase with the convex-trimesh multi-manifold patch correlator (convexMeshCorrelate.cu correlate + its purgeEmptyTriangles prelude): the purge compaction storing tempCounts = (origSlot &lt;&lt; 4) | getNbContacts with FOUR count bits that the patch loop reads back as THREE (index &amp; 7 — an 8-contact triangle purges as valid, contributes nothing, is never accepted and never cleared, running the loop out to its four manifolds); the zero-triangle early-out writing outBuffer[pairIndex] while every other write goes to outBuffer[pair.cmIndex]; the deepest-triangle scan (per-lane strided strict &lt;, lowest-lane exact min over whole-word tempCounts candidates); strict-&gt; PATCH_ACCEPTANCE_EPS acceptance; the contact pull loop (readMask = ~currentContactMask, last-index binarySearch over exclusive run-sums — with zero-contact duplicates the contact-bearing lane is provably the last of each equal-runsum group — c advanced by popc(readMask), contactReduce&lt;clustering,6&gt; only at strictly more than six held contacts, lane state persisting across blocks and rounds); and per-patch output at scan slots with empty patches included and the nbPatches-gated mRelativeTransform write.  Verified over 3000 fixtures (cache-hit, zero-triangle, cmIndex slot split, two-block sets, multi-round pulls, the 4-bit/3-bit quirk, empty patches, exact cross-lane and strided depth ties, an acceptance dot landing bitwise ON the epsilon, and an exactly-6-held duplicate-anchor state where a spurious reduce would collapse clusters) — bit-exact on the first functional run; 21-mutation battery: 21 caught after two initially-surviving strict-&gt; boundary mutants were pinned by the rigged exact-boundary fixtures; the adversarial review confirmed the port bit-exact with no divergences and its three test-hardening notes were applied (mNbContacts sentinel-init so the empty-patch write is observable — with a regression mutant — plus exercised-quirk and reduce-ran coverage counters) (two other drafted mutants were replaced pre-run as provably equivalent: the pull-loop c overshoot is unobservable past loop exit, and rejected lanes' kept startIndex is never read since binarySearch always lands on the contact-bearing lane).  `npCvxPostProcess.slang` ports both convexMeshPostProcess cores (convexTrimeshPostProcessCore + convexHeightfieldPostProcessCore), the scalar second-pass silhouette culling: the pairIdx (&amp; 0x1FFFFFFF) / voronoi-mask (&amp; 0xE0000000) word decode, the getNbContacts early-out, the real trimesh byte-offset blob walk (header, hdr.w × 1168-byte BV32DataPacked skip, verts, triIndices, adjacencies, faceRemap, accumulated triangle refs, references), flipNormal (negative scale determinant) swapping adjacency x↔z and index y↔z, the six voronoi branches with their vestigial-but-kept guards (isBoundaryEdge on a non-0xFFFFFFFF neighbour; isActiveEdge on heightfield adjacencies, which never carry the NONCONVEX bit) and the un-stripped bit 31 that makes flagged neighbours never match the ordered-table search, the wrongHanded out-argument permutations feeding the tranche-59 heightfield helpers, the clamped 3×3 cell walk (including the harmless beyond-last-row phantom visit — phantom triangle ids exceed the ordered domain), and the final index &amp;= 0x0FFFFFF clear whose six F's (0x00FFFFFF) also zero triangle-index bits 24-26.  Verified integer-exact over 6000 fixtures (both modes, boundary and NONCONVEX-flagged neighbours, BV32-offset blobs, flipped/wrong-handed scales, unknown voronoi masks falling through to a clear, bits 24-26 pinning the clear width) — bit-exact on the first functional run; ctest 456/456; 23-mutation battery: 23 caught (four equivalent drafts replaced before/after analysis: high pairIdx bits vanish under the model's 4-entry pairs table, no fixture scale determinant is exactly zero, the isActiveEdge guard is provably dead, and the phantom row visit is unobservable).  The adversarial review then caught a REAL transcription bug the test could not see because its reference shared it: the voronoi enum declares eE02 = 5&lt;&lt;29 BEFORE eE12 = 6&lt;&lt;29 (declaration order, not the cores' branch order), so the port had the two edge masks swapped, taking the wrong adjacency for genuinely reachable second-pass words — fixed in shader and reference with a regression mutant pinning the constant order.  `npCvxTrimeshOut.slang` completes the convex-trimesh pipeline's output with convexTrimeshFinishContacts + writeCompressedContact: the lane/6 manifold layout, the lane-0 reservation with CHAINED else-if overflow tests at FULL reservation sizes (vs the plane kernel's independent single-element tests) and its rollback, the insertAveragePoint inflation that lives on lane 0 only — making the faceIndex region base per-lane divergent, with lane 0's slot able to collide with another lane's (a genuine unordered intra-warp CUDA write conflict, masked from comparison) while the reserved average-contact slots are never written (getAvgContactValue is dead code) — the no-reservation path storing base + 0xFFFFFFFF wrapped pointers yet passing the early-return gate, the flip swap with patch-normal negation, the first-contact worldNormal, the sphere/capsule radius adjustments, and the 64-byte PxContactPatch image (header-verified byte offsets: mass modification, normal/restitution, frictions/damping, startContactIndex|nbContacts&lt;&lt;16|matFlags&lt;&lt;24, eHAS_FACE_INDICES|matIdx0&lt;&lt;16) via the tranche-43 combineMaterials.  Verified over 6000 fixtures (all three chained overflows plus a both-tight fixture pinning the chain order, exact fits, zero/empty manifolds, lane-0-contactless manifolds, wrapped-pointer no-reservation, inflation conflicts, flip and sphere/capsule paths): integers exact, rotated floats to tight tolerance — bit-exact on the first functional run; ctest 459/459; 23-mutation battery: 23 caught; the adversarial review confirmed the port with NO divergences (independently re-deriving the PxContactPatch byte image, the combineMaterials out-parameter mapping, the L257 gate equivalence and the exactness of the conflict-word mask).  `npTriTriOverlap.slang` ports triangleTriangleOverlaps, the SDF-free mesh-mesh overlap marking kernel: root BV32 node bounds of both meshes reduced with exact fminf/fmaxf over the (1 &lt;&lt; mNbNodes) − 1 lane mask, the eight AABB corners per mesh (a set lane bit selects the MIN component), vertex2Shape scaling, the A side moved into B space via aToB = t1.transformInv(t0), the initialDir fallback (0,1,0) at zero squared distance, convergenceRatio = 1 − 0.000225, and the tranche-129 cold-start GJK over the two 8-point clouds copied verbatim (the prep writes the clouds into the same scratch buffers the driver reads); overlaps = result ≠ eDISTANT.  The headline result: the ENTIRE GJK iteration is bit-reproducible on arbitrary non-integer inputs — lowerBound/upperBound compare bitwise across all 4000 non-marginal fixtures (overlap/close/distant bands, identical transforms pinning the fallback, multi-node bounds), with a symmetric margin skip only on the DISTANT decision boundary; ctest 461/461; 18-mutation battery: 18 caught (two equivalent drafts replaced pre-run — the DISTANT-gate strictness is margin-hidden by design and upperBound is a magnitude).  The adversarial review then caught a real 1-ulp fidelity divergence the test was blind to because its reference shared it: the new quatMul helper regrouped the w component as w·w − (x+y+z) while PxQuat subtracts sequentially — fixed in shader and reference (every earlier tranche already used the sequential form), pinned by a regression mutant, with three further review-proposed mutants (mesh1 loop bound, per-side scale pairing, aToB.p operand order) all caught, and the prep-point comparison hardened from tolerance to bitwise, checked on all fixtures including margin-skipped ones.  The sibling triangleTriangleCollision is deliberately NOT ported: its core calls doTriangleSDFCollision over tex3D SDF textures — hardware filtering, the same non-portable category as sdfCollision.cuh (the earlier scoping note listed it as portable; it is not).  `npCvxTriNpSetup.slang` ports the convexTrimeshNarrowphaseCore setup stage (everything before the hand-off into the tranche-96/110/112 convexTriangleContactGen pipeline): the trimeshToConvexTransform composition, the contact-distance sum, the convex blob head (center of mass through vertex2Shape with the convex scale plus the packed counts word), the readTriangleMesh byte walk, the pair's adjacency row and faceRemap entry, the three triangle vertices with the negative-determinant writeIndex permutation (threads 1/2 swap via tid ^ 3, flipping the winding), the local normal over the permuted vertices, and the startIndex/roundedStartIndex offsets.  Bit-exact on the first functional run over 6000 fixtures — and the test compares ALL outputs bitwise (the tranche-159 GJK result proved the framework bit-identical through quaternion chains, multiplies and normalizations, so tolerance comparison is retired for pure-transform tranches, catching 1-ulp association drifts by construction); ctest 463/463; 16-mutation battery: 16 caught after one fixture gap was closed (a determinant mutant dropping the y component survived while no fixture generated a negative y scale — all three axes now go negative independently).  `npCvxHfNpSetup.slang` ports the convexHeightfieldNarrowphaseCore setup stage (convexHeightfield.cu, the heightfield sibling of npCvxTriNpSetup): getTriangle with wrongHanded = (columnScale&lt;0) XOR (rowScale&lt;0) (the handedness vector is dead code), the [0, 1+wh, 2&minus;wh] vertex and [wh?2:0, 1, wh?0:2] adjacency out-argument permutations over the tranche-59 helpers (0x800000 tessellation bit, sign-extended 16-bit heights, HF_BOUNDARY edges), the hf2shapep per-component multiply, the denormalizedNormal (v1&minus;v0)&times;(v2&minus;v0) rotated ONLY then normalized, the fully transformed local verts, and the shared transform/contactDist/CoM/offset prep.  Bit-exact on the first functional run over 6000 fixtures (all sign combinations of row/height/column scale, tessellated and non-tessellated cells, boundary and interior adjacency), ALL outputs compared bitwise; ctest 465/465; 20-mutation battery: 20 caught after one domain-equivalent draft was replaced pre-run (cell &ge; columns&minus;1 &equiv; cell &ge; columns on valid cells, since a cell's column is &le; columns&minus;2).  The tranche-160 adversarial review confirmed npCvxTriNpSetup faithful (no divergence) but flagged a real coverage gap: the blob-walk sizes never exceeded nbPacked=1 / numTris=8 / numVerts=12, letting four cap/saturate mutants (min(nbPacked,1), boolean skip, numTris cap, numVerts &amp; 15) survive; the fixture now reaches nbPacked=2 and numVerts/numTris up to 20 within blob[192], and all four regression mutants are killed.  `npCvxCoreOut.slang` opens the convexCoreCollision.cu family (the PxConvexCoreGeometry narrowphase, the last bit-exact-portable CUDA source) with its shared output stage, the two TestOutput::writeOutput overloads: ccoWriteContacts (the PxContact-array variant of the plane and convex-convex kernels) and ccoWriteContact (the Gu::Contact variant of the mesh/heightfield/cloth kernels).  Both carry the single-element 64-bit-promoted overflow tests, the wrapped-0xFFFFFFFF force offset stored as-is, the allflags/touch/patch-change rewrites and the patch rollbacks; the Contact variant adds the patch-major sequential contact stream, the numPoints-vs-numPatches gate split (status and reservations gate on numPoints, currentlyHasTouch on numPatches &mdash; ported verbatim, NOT normalized), the numPatches&times;64-byte reservation, and the INLINE PxContactPatch array write (mass 1.0f&times;4, normal, restitution/frictions/damping from the tranche-43 combineMaterials core reproduced verbatim, accumulating startContactIndex, materialFlags u8, internalFlags 0) &mdash; no insertIntoPatchStream on that path.  Bit-exact on the first functional run, 2&times;4000 fixtures (zero-contact, exactly-tight single-element quirks, high-region 64-bit compares, up to 16 patches with 1&ndash;4 points each, compliant/acc-spring/friction-disable material combos), ALL outputs compared bitwise; 22/22 mutants killed.  (The tranche-161 adversarial review confirmed npCvxHfNpSetup with NO divergence and 30/30 mutants killed &mdash; the sample-word layout was verified against PxHeightFieldSample.h/PxBitAndData.h: tess flag = bit 23, heights = sign-extended low 16 bits.)  `npCvxCoreSetup.slang` adds the convex-core kernel setup glue: the TestInput constructor (contactDist sum, restDistance-NULL guard) with checkTypes' reverse-match swap of shapes/cacheRefs/transforms, Gu::makeConvexShape's five core mappings into the 40-byte Gu::ConvexShape coreData image (eCONVEXCORE's 24-byte memcpy from &amp;scale.scale.x with margin = rotation.w and the enum carried in the hull pointer; sphere&rarr;ePOINT margin; capsule&rarr;SegmentCore length&times;2; box&rarr;BoxCore extents&times;2; convex mesh&rarr;PointsCore with points = blob+3 float4s, numPoints = (header &gt;&gt; 8) &amp; 0xff, stride 16) plus the Enum(&minus;1) unsupported-type path, and the per-kernel preps: the plane kernel's PxPlane from getBasisVector0 (exact PxQuat.h term order) and the convex-convex mid-point shift.  Bit-exact on the first functional run, 2&times;4000 fixtures, ALL outputs bitwise; 18-mutation battery: 18 caught after one provably-equivalent draft was replaced pre-run ((a+b)&times;0.5 vs a&times;0.5+b&times;0.5 is bit-identical &mdash; &times;0.5 is exact and /2 commutes with IEEE rounding &mdash; so the killable mutant attacks the pose1 subtraction instead).  The tranche-162 adversarial review confirmed npCvxCoreOut faithful (no divergence; the PxContactPatch layout, merge() packing and combineMaterials verified header-for-header) but demonstrated that the high-region 64-bit-promotion fixtures only covered the CONTACT counter and that the coupled numPatches/numPoints fixtures could not pin the gate split: the force/patch counters now also reach 0xFFFFFFxx, decoupled patch/point pairs (patches without points, points without patches) are injected, and all five review survivors are killed as regression mutants.  `npCvxCoreSupport.slang` ports the Gu::ConvexCore localSupport family for all seven core types plus the Gu::ConvexShape support wrappers (support = pose.transform(supportLocal(pose.rotateInv(dir))) + dir&times;margin; computeBounds from six axis supports): PxSign's &ge;0 convention on RAW dirs for segment/box, the ellipsoid diagonal transform-transpose&rarr;normalize&rarr;transform, the cylinder/cone normalized-dir branches (1e-5 degenerate radial caps, the cone sinA = r/&radic;(h&sup2;+r&sup2;) apex test), and the ePOINTS argmax over the S/R vertex2shape form with the NULL-pool and |d| &lt; FLT_EPSILON guards and a stride-honoring pool walk.  Bit-exact over 7000 fixtures (all seven types, exact-axis degenerate dirs, tiny-S magnitudes landing in (0, FLT_EPSILON), stride-32 pools, NULL/empty pools) with ALL outputs bitwise; 18/18 mutants killed after two fixture rounds (the first tiny-S draft at 1e-25 underflowed the squared magnitude to exactly zero, making the &lt;FLT_EPSILON and &le;0 guards indistinguishable &mdash; 1e-8 separates them; and an out-of-bounds pool read in the stride-32 fixture was corrected before the battery).  The tranche-163 adversarial review confirmed npCvxCoreSetup faithful (PxgShape/PxMeshScale contiguity of the 24-byte memcpy and the signed-shift numPoints extraction verified header-for-header, no divergence); its two passthrough-width survivors (hullLow beyond 3 bits, cacheRefs beyond 10 bits) are closed with full-width fixtures and killed regression mutants.  `npCvxCoreFace.slang` ports the Gu::ConvexCore contactFace family for the six analytic core types plus rotatePoints and the ConvexShape::contactFace wrapper (local dir/point via rotateInv/transformInv, world rotate of the face normal, facePoints pushed by faceNormal&times;margin): the 0.99/0.14 threshold branches, the box per-axis loop with its corner sign layout and the degeneracy outs, the ellipsoid branches that REQUIRE degenerate radii, the cylinder/cone cap-vs-side split (cone: sinA apex frame, dX rotated-angle band, (r/h, dr.y, dr.z) side normal), and rotatePoints' division-form normalize() guards (PxVec3::normalize is v/m -- a bitwise-distinct form from getNormalized's v&times;(1/&radic;m), and the battery proves the test can tell them apart).  NEW COMPARE CLASS: rotatePoints runs acos/sin/cos, which are not bit-exact primitives (Vulkan permits sin/cos error up to 2^-11 and CUDA's own acosf differs in ulps from any libm), so ONLY the cap-branch facePoints compare to tolerance (2e-3 + 1e-3&times;rel, NaN==NaN) behind a deterministic trig flag -- every branch decision, count, face normal and every non-trig output stays bitwise (per the tranche-155b acos precedent).  9000 fixtures (local-frame constructed dirs: in-cone, in-band, exact-axis, random; zero extents/radii/heights driving every degeneracy out); 17/17 mutants killed.  The tranche-164 adversarial review found the series' fourth test-blind finding, and the first of a NEW class: npCvxCoreSupport's ellipsoid localSupport used a bare per-component multiply where the CUDA compiles PxMat33::createDiagonal + transformTranspose/transform, whose explicit zero terms are signed-zero exact (0&times;negative = &minus;0, (&minus;0)+(+0) = +0) &mdash; and the exact form turned out to be UNENFORCEABLE on the verification backend: slangc/llvmpipe fold the &plusmn;0 additions away (no-signed-zeros), proven live even with the zero smuggled in as runtime data.  Resolution: the CPU reference now carries the true matrix form, the shader keeps the multiply (identical in every magnitude bit), and the ellipsoid compare alone is zero-sign-insensitive (f2u equality OR both-zero) &mdash; signed-zero semantics are documented as out of verification scope for this backend, with no downstream sensitivity (dots and PxSign(&ge;0) ignore &minus;0).  The review's second finding &mdash; the ePOINTS NULL check inspected only the low pointer word &mdash; is fixed to 64 bits in both the support and (pre-commit) points-face shaders.  Its coverage gaps are closed with new fixtures (signed-zero and all-zero dirs, cylinder radial band just above the 1e-5 eps, exact argmax ties via identity-frame equal-projection point pairs, |d| between FLT_EPSILON and &radic;FLT_EPSILON separating the sqrt'd guard, cone dirs landing d.x EXACTLY on sinA by nextafter search, and 64-bit pointers with a zero low word) and five killed regression mutants; the full 23-mutant battery reruns green.  `npCvxCorePtsFace.slang` completes the contactFace family with Gu::reducePolygon (farthest-pair seeding with keep1st, the strict &plusmn;FLT_EPSILON acceptance bands starting at ZERO, the duplicate-filtered pi0/pi1/pi2/pi3 emit) and the ePOINTS contactFace (three-max swap cascade in pool order, the normal-candidate chain, the coplanar 1e-4 accumulation loop with head-swap and repeated keep1st reduce) &mdash; all sqrt/div-only, ALL outputs bitwise; 2&times;6000 fixtures (coplanar polygons, duplicates, all-identical pools, tiny pools of 1&ndash;3, tilted top triangles, near-band offsets, 64-bit hi-word pointers); 17/17 mutants killed after three provably-equivalent drafts were replaced with domain proofs: the pi2/pi3 dedup guards are dead (the two band extremes are disjoint and d(pi0) = +0 exactly), the 2-point candidate acceptance is dead code in the SOURCE (dir&middot;((dir&times;e)&times;e) &le; 0 by Cauchy-Schwarz &mdash; it can never pass its own &gt;0.99 test), and the final numPoints&ge;2 gate is dead because every accepted normal candidate is orthogonal to a top edge, putting &ge;2 points in its own band.  MEANWHILE the tranche-165 adversarial review found test-blind divergences FIVE and SIX: PxVec3::normalize compiles to operator/= which is ONE division then three multiplies (v &times; (1/m)) &mdash; the port and reference both used per-component division, mutually masked, 362/9000 fixtures' 2-point faceNormals differed bitwise from the true form &mdash; and PxAcos CLAMPS its argument to [&minus;1,1] where the port would NaN.  Both fixed on both sides with regression mutants; the normalize-form mutant kills, the clamp mutant is a DOCUMENTED EQUIVALENT (the clamp is dead defensive code at its only call site: dir0 is single-component from the cap layout, so the acos argument is fl(d0y&middot;d1y) &le; 1 always &mdash; probes found 0/5.2M over-unit results from correctly-rounded normalize chains).  New fixtures fire the rotatePoints dir1/axis FLT_EPSILON guards (a one-ulp ref offset beats the axis-guard masking of the exact-zero case).  `npCvxCoreClip.slang` ports Gu::FaceClipper behind generateContactPatch &mdash; the contact-patch builder the convex-core kernels feed with GJKEPA output: the clipNone/clip2x2/clipNxN routing, clip2x2's 1e-5 parallel gate, four twin-gated endpoint projections in PxVec3::operator/'s reciprocal-multiply form ((v&times;s)&times;(1/m), NOT v&times;(s/m) &mdash; C++ precedence applies the vector division AFTER the scalar multiply), and the backwards duplicate merge keeping the smaller w; clipNxN's makePlanes (per-edge-pair cross&times;axis with the 1e-10 viability floor, unguarded normalizeFast, the side-count skip/flip), makePolygon (PxComputeBasisVectors' 0.9999 branch with the special up construction, the XY AABB rebased at the GJKEPA midpoint), clipPoly's -1e-5 keep band with entry/exit crossing lerps, the post-clip midpoint append, the two face-plane axis projections, the min-w anchor swap and the PxVec4 reducePolygon call site.  Bit-exact over 8000 fixtures with ALL outputs bitwise; 22/22 mutants killed after threshold fixtures were added (near-parallel twists in (1e-5, 1e-3), a face edge nearly along the axis pinning the 1e-10 floor, |axis.y| in (0.5, 0.9999)) and one zero-sign-equivalent draft was replaced (cross(axis, X) rewrite differs from the special up form only in the signs of exact zeros since X.y is &plusmn;0 &mdash; NSZ zone).  `npCvxCoreContact.slang` ports Gu::Contact, the patch accumulator the convex-core mesh kernels drive point-by-point: addPoint's first-match 0.999 same-normal cone (the &ge; comparison pinned by a fixture landing the dot EXACTLY on 0.999f via unnormalized axis inputs), addPatch's at-capacity replacement (max single dot vs the closest existing pair, dropping the new patch or replacing the SHALLOWER pair member by min-depth), and addPatchPoint's prepend + depth bubble (BREAK at the first non-swap) + keep1st reducePolygon over (p,d) points with the mNumPoints rebalance.  All dot/compare arithmetic, ALL outputs bitwise over 6000 fixtures (normal clusters inside/outside the cone, 16-patch saturation with replacement and drop paths, 4-point reduces); 13/14 mutants killed &mdash; the one survivor (bubble break removed) is distinguishable only when a distance TIE in the farthest scan flips first-wins after the full bubble reorders slots behind the break point; handed to the adversarial review with the construction sketch.  The tranche-166 adversarial review confirmed npCvxCorePtsFace faithful but OVERTURNED two of the three tranche-166 domain proofs: the pi1 dedup guards in reducePolygon are LOAD-BEARING (band rounding ~1e-5 exceeds FLT_EPSILON; pi2==pi1/pi3==pi1 occur hundreds of times in the fixtures &mdash; the port keeps the guards and their removal is killed), and the numPoints&ge;2 gate is LIVE (reducePolygon returns 1 when the band clusters within &radic;FLT_EPSILON &asymp; 3.45e-4 &mdash; 17k/100k probe cases, reachable with sub-0.35mm vertex clusters); only the Cauchy-Schwarz dead-code proof stands verified.  Its four eps-window coverage gaps are closed with tight-cluster and near-collinear reduce fixtures plus a tight-top-cluster pointsFace fixture that fires the live gate, and three killed regression mutants (gate removal, both reduce eps drops).  The combined tranche-167/168 adversarial review confirmed both ports faithful (the FaceClipper's reciprocal-multiply division form verified against PxVec3::operator/, every clip2x2/makePlanes/clipPoly gate and the Contact capacity bookkeeping expression-checked) and surfaced one latent SOURCE bug, mirrored as-is: MAX_CLIP_PLANES = 8 only suffices for faces in strictly convex position &mdash; the &le;1e-5 side-count band lets degenerate (sliver/collinear) faces emit up to 6 planes each (12 total), overflowing clipPlanes[8] in the CUDA source itself (stack OOB, UB; probe max over the fixtures is exactly 8, and the overflow is untestable bitwise, so it is documented rather than masked).  The review RESOLVED the tranche-168 m5 survivor as NOT equivalent: a 4-add construction (the reduce-gather leaves the pool depth-unsorted, the next add breaks the bubble at i=0, and an exact farthest-distance tie 9+16 == 25+0 flips first-wins once the mutant's full pass reorders the tail) now lives in the fixtures and kills it, joined by two exact-tie regression mutants (bitwise-equal pair min-depths must replace pi1; two bitwise-equal pair dots must keep the FIRST pair) &mdash; all three killed.  `npCvxCoreGjk.slang` ports Gu::RefGjkEpa::computeGjkDistance whole (GuRefGjkEpa.h L44-419) over the margin-less ConvexShape::supportLocal: the Convex&lt;Support&gt; weld cache (MAX_VERTS 32, accuracy = max(1% local extent, FLT_EPSILON), BACKWARD weld scan with early-out, strict closest tracking, full-cache closest fallback), GjkDistance (bounds-center seed with the (1,0,0) fallback; addPoint's 100-iteration cap and duplicate-(aI,bI) FLT_MAX out; the four Johnson computeClosest cases transcribed expression-for-expression incl. the abc&middot;a &gt; 0 swap and the |closest| &lt; |mClosest| &minus; FLT_EPSILON improvement test with the &minus;&minus;mNumVerts backtrack) and the driver loop (distEps = 0.01&middot;min accuracy; 0 / dist+points+axis / FLT_MAX exits) &mdash; ALL outputs bitwise (modulo the tranche-164 &plusmn;0 NSZ exception class) over 4000 fixtures: overlap/gap/beyond-maxDist/graze classes across all 7&times;7 core-type pairs, DISJOINT asymmetric ePOINTS simplex hulls with EXACTLY equal bounds centers (the seed fallback fires on a SEPARATED pair whose parallel closest faces make the fallback choice observable) plus a y-dominant 5e-5 nudge variant pinning the FLT_EPSILON threshold from the non-fallback side, and three hill-climbed razor ellipsoid pairs (scratchpad gjkprobe3/5, exact bits embedded): one filling the weld cache to all 32 slots, one whose full-cache fallback executes LIVE mid-trajectory (pinning closest tracking AND the fallback index), and one with maxDist planted at 0.7&middot;D inside the proj ramp (the tag-2/tag-1 razor).  25/26 mutants killed; m18 (backtrack removal) is PROVEN output-equivalent: after a non-improvement pass dir is frozen, so the next addPoint re-queries the same support pair and BOTH variants exit tag-1 with identical closestDist/axis (mutant: immediate duplicate; faithful: duplicate or an iteration-cap spin re-pushing the identical vert with constant proj), computePoints differing only by an s=0 vert's exact &plusmn;0 contribution.  The tranche-169 adversarial review CONFIRMED the GJK port faithful line-by-line (all foundation forms re-verified against the PhysX headers, all four Johnson cases term-for-term), VERIFIED the m18 backtrack equivalence proof (including the shared-weld-cache attack: the faithful spin's re-query welds to the identical index and never grows the cache), and ran four extra mutants (case-4 vertex/edge/dca-face shuffles + the cylinder cap tuple) &mdash; all KILLED.  Its findings are fixed forward: the test reference's cylinder/cone radial branches now carry CUDA's literal `0 +` adds (the D1 test-blind &plusmn;0 hazard: dropping them hid a -0-vs-+0 divergence class from the eqFZ compare; the residual llvmpipe NSZ risk now sits on the shader side where a mismatch is visible), type-coverage counters count what actually DISPATCHES rather than the pre-override RNG draw, the near-vacuous graze counter left the assert, and the two unreached supportLocal branches got fixtures and killed regression mutants (ePOINTS 64-bit-NULL pointer image; the |d| &lt; FLT_EPSILON degenerate-scale guard via S &asymp; 1e-8 cores) &mdash; 27/28 killed + m18 proven.  `npCvxCoreEpa.slang` (tranche 170) ports Gu::RefGjkEpa::computeEpaDepth whole (GuRefGjkEpa.h L421-699): the EpaDepth ctor (bounds-center seed, PxComputeBasisVectors per PxMathUtils.h L297-316 incl. the |dir.y| &gt; 0.9999 else-branch, conditional &plusmn;dir1/&plusmn;dir2 seeding while mNumVerts &lt; 3, the post-seed mProj reset), addPoint (MAX_VERTS cap; the mProj/mBest update BEFORE the duplicate FLT_MAX out; the two seed faces at numVerts == 3; the BACKWARD face-removal scan with reversed-twin edge cancellation and remove-by-swap; the numEdges == 0 and MAX_FACES &minus; numEdges outs; the new-face fan), makePlane's symmetrized triple-cross with normalEps and the reciprocal-multiply normalization, computeClosest's strict plane.w argmax with the numFaces == 2 mBest latch, computePoints' barycentric split accumulation, and the driver.  PORTING DISCOVERY, new exception-class infrastructure: llvmpipe CONTRACTS the makePlane triple-cross sums into fused fma chains (verified by exact re-computation: the GPU value equals a full 6-product fma chain, 1 ulp off strict IEEE) &mdash; legal per Vulkan since slangc emits no NoContraction decorations and its -fp-mode precise flag is a no-op for SPIR-V.  New tool `tools/spv_nocontract.py` post-processes the .spv (wired via PXG_NOCONTRACT_SHADERS in the tests CMake), stamping NoContraction on every float arithmetic op; with it the EPA suite is fully bitwise (modulo the &plusmn;0 class) over 4000 fixtures (overlap depth classes, graze, separated, identical-pose fallback, &plusmn;y-aligned centers driving the basis else-branch, CORE_POINT isValid-false pairs, NULL/degenerate-S ePOINTS).  Mutation battery 26/29 killed; m16 (numEdges==0 out), m17 (+m17b threshold variant; MAX_FACES overflow out) and m20 (numFaces==2 latch) are PROVEN output-equivalent in Euler-consistent polytope states (closed-convex no-total-removal argument; F = 2V&minus;4 caps faces at 60 during the vert-32 pass and every later exit consumes the frozen mBest since the vert-cap out precedes the mProj update; first-pass latch redundancy) with the residual confined to eps-degenerate Euler-violating states, documented in the harness.  The tranche-170 adversarial review confirmed the EPA port and its CPU transcription faithful (no divergences; the spv_nocontract tool audited correct, incl. the OpFNegate/ExtInst non-contractibility arguments and a proof that the driver guard bound is inert at exactly 30 passes) but REFUTED three of the four claimed mutant-equivalence proofs &mdash; the second time eps-arithmetic has overturned geometric domain proofs: (a) numEdges==0-out: with a CORE_POINT partner accuracy = FLT_EPSILON makes testEps ~1.2e-9, below dot rounding noise, so a point can land above BOTH faces of the 2-face state and remove ALL faces in an Euler-CONSISTENT polytope (2715/200k in thin-geometry families, 65 output-divergent); (b) the MAX_FACES overflow guard fires for DISCONNECTED visible regions (numFaces + numEdges = F + 2C &minus; 2I &gt; 60) in the test's own fixture distribution at other seeds (~0.05%), with real OOB writes in the mutant; (c) the threshold variant m17b diverges in the sum&isin;{59,60}, numVerts&lt;32 region.  All three are now KILLED by embedded bit-exact kill fixtures from the review's fuzzer, plus a new collinear-ePOINTS-vs-point fixture exercising the previously untested two-seed-face makePlane FLT_MAX path (planeFail now asserted &ge;1, faceOver &ge;1).  Only m20 (the numFaces==2 mBest latch) stands as a VERIFIED equivalence: first-pass redundancy is rigorous and no 2-face recurrence appeared in 700k adversarial fuzz samples.  Battery final: 28/31 killed + 1 verified-equivalent (m20) + m16/m17/m17b resolved-by-fixture.  `npCvxCorePlaneGen.slang` (tranche 171a) ports Gu::generateContacts(PxPlane, ConvexShape) (GuConvexSupport.cpp L41-75), COMPOSED from the individually battle-tested transcriptions: the margin-carrying ConvexShape::support (tranche 164), the full contactFace family &mdash; six analytic types + rotatePoints (tranche 165) and the ePOINTS hull-face reduce (tranche 166) &mdash; behind the ConvexShape wrapper (local-frame prologue, world rotate + margin push epilogue), and the driver: normal = &minus;plane.n, dist = point1&middot;n + d gate against contactDist, the single midpoint contact when contactFace returns 0 points, else per-face-point p1 + normal&middot;d&middot;0.5 with recomputed plane distances.  Compare doctrine inherited exactly: counts/normal/non-trig outputs BITWISE (mod &plusmn;0), cylinder/cone cap points behind the deterministic trig flag to tolerance; proactively wired into PXG_NOCONTRACT_SHADERS.  6000 fixtures (hit/miss bands, straddling poses, axis-aligned planes, identity-pose cap fixtures driving rotatePoints 496&times;, degenerate radii/extents, all 7 core types); driver mutation battery 12/12 killed (normal sign, margin adds on both sides, plane-distance form, gate, midpoint factor and branch, per-point distance recompute, wrapper prologue/epilogue forms, local-frame rotation, dists slot) &mdash; the contactFace internals carry the tranche-165/166 batteries unchanged.  Remaining in the family: the convex&times;convex generateContacts driver (GuConvexSupport.h L1162-1207: GJK &rarr; EPA &rarr; margin/cull &rarr; generateContactPatch), tranche 171b.  `npCvxCoreCvxGen.slang` (tranche 171b) completes the convex-core contact family: the full convex-vs-convex Gu::generateContacts driver (GuConvexSupport.h L1162-1207), COMPOSED verbatim from the battle-tested transcriptions as functions in one shader &mdash; gjkDistanceQ (tranche 169), epaDepthQ (tranche 170), the contactFace wrapper family (165/166) and faceClipQ (167) &mdash; glued by the driver: maxDist = margin0 + margin1 + contactDist, GJK, EPA when dist &lt; FLT_EPSILON, the dist &gt; maxDist and cullDir&middot;axis &lt; &minus;1e-5 rejects, the margin pushback of point0/point1, and generateContactPatch (the FaceClipper ctor's two world-space contactFace calls + clip + getContact).  Wired into PXG_NOCONTRACT_SHADERS.  The CPU reference composes the four tranche references VERBATIM in separate namespaces (gjkref/eparef/faceref/clipref) plus the driver glue.  3000 fixtures (overlap &rarr; EPA path, graze, contactDist window, clear miss, live cull dirs, identity-pose cap-contact trig cases); 2688 non-trig cases verify BITWISE (modulo &plusmn;0), trig-flagged cases to tolerance.  NEW DOCUMENTED COMPARE CLASS: in trig-flagged cases the rotatePoints acos/sin/cos deviation (not bit-exact-portable on CUDA itself) can flip a downstream FaceClipper band or reduce decision, changing the emitted point COUNT or ORDER &mdash; such razor cases are excluded from the bitwise compare, counted, and CAPPED at 8 of 3000 (observed 6).  Driver mutation battery pending next window alongside the combined 171a/171b adversarial review.  The combined tranche-171a/171b adversarial review PASSED with no divergences: every composition seam diffed mechanically identical to the tranche shaders/references, the driver glue verified line-by-line (incl. the testEps short-circuit ordering, the dead adjusted-dist, and the FaceClipper ctor sign/argument order), all memcpy struct adaptations audited, 4/4 extra seam mutants killed, and all 5 trig-unstable exclusions instrumented as genuinely trig-origin (angle nudges of &plusmn;5e-4 rad reproduce the count/order flips).  Its hardening is applied: the trig-unstable exclusion now additionally requires bitwise-equal trig flags and normal (which trig cannot alter, so a flag-plumbing or EPA-axis bug can no longer hide under the cap &mdash; the review's X3 mutant demonstrated the cap was load-bearing), and the emit tag joined the compare.  `npCvxBvhGlue.slang` (tranche 172) ports the TrimeshBvh scalar glue (convexCoreCollision.cu L548-682): scaleBounds = PxBounds3::transformFast(scale.getInverse().toMat33(), bounds) via basisExtent's abs-sum row extents, the traversal-callback margin dims.minElement()&middot;1e-4, the PxBounds3::intersects six-comparison box test, and createConvex's mesh-scale-transformed vertices + the ePOINTS PointsCore image (tranche-163 packing; the TetmeshBvh variant is the same image with numPoints = 4 and NO scale transform &mdash; arithmetic-free repacking, documented here).  getTriNormal/getTriBarycentric were already ported as tranche 71 (npTriMesh), warp reducePoints as tranche 70 (npConvexReduce), bv32TreeTraversal as tranche 76.  8000 fixtures (identity/uniform/non-uniform/NEGATIVE scale components, exact face-touch boxes pinning the strict > in intersects); ALL outputs bitwise; battery 10/10 killed.  Remaining in the family: the Contact32 warp accumulator (addPoints/copyPoints/findCreatePatch, tranche 173), after which the five __global__ kernels are dispatcher/atomics glue over ported units.  `npCvxCoreWarpAcc.slang` (tranche 173) ports Gu::Contact32, the full-warp contact-patch accumulator of convexCoreCollision.cu (L686-917) and the LAST unported compute core of the convex-core family: addPoints + copyPoints + the warp reducePoints + findCreatePatch, with the warp collectives emulated as deterministic lane loops over explicit 32-bit masks preserving CUDA's conventions exactly &mdash; the inclusive warpScan prefix layout with per-lane rotated normals, lowest-lane-wins minIndex/maxIndex, ballot masks, the two-stage reducePoints (deepest/farthest/two side extremes, duplicate-collapsing pick masks, popcount write offsets, the deepest-to-slot-0 swap), and findCreatePatch's strict &gt; SAME_NORMAL match / append / closest-pair drop-replace at capacity keyed on slot-0 dists.  3000 fixtures (normal clusters vs 18+ distinct directions filling all 16 patches 787&times;, two sequential warp calls, sparse lanes, cross-lane exact depth ties, and a review-168-pattern exact farthest-distance tie 9+16 == 25+0 with side candidates whose pick sets DIVERGE under the tie &mdash; needed because with fewer points both tie choices produce the same pick mask); ALL outputs bitwise. The adversarial review of tranches 172/173 CONFIRMED both faithful line-by-line: the PointsCore image verified word-for-word against GuConvexSupport.h L354 and the tranche-163 consumer, margin correctly from the SCALED bounds (an extra mutant x1 probing the unscaled-bounds trap was killed), the warp emulation checked against the REAL nputils.cuh L137-165 (minIndex/maxIndex = ffs-of-ballot, lowest-lane-wins on ties &mdash; exactly the emulation's ascending strict-compare loops, incl. mixed &plusmn;0), warpScan confirmed INCLUSIVE (reduction.cuh L260-283), and the port's persistent removed-lane values proven unobservable through the ballot masks (CUDA flips them to a dummy each iteration; every consumer masks them out).  m9's proof upheld; m8's proof upheld with corrected reasoning (the reversed cross is only COMPARISON-equivalent to a negation &mdash; exact cancellations round to +0 in both variants, and IEEE compares are zero-sign-blind); m10's proof REFUTED: the zero-init argument only covers slots [mNumPatches, 16), while the lane loop runs to 31 and patchNormal has 16 elements &mdash; the mutant emits an unclamped OpAccessChain reading patchNormal[16..31] out of bounds (UB per SPIR-V, disassembly-confirmed), so its survival is driver-accident, not equivalence; reclassified from proven-equivalent to survives-by-UB / not provable (the shipped port keeps the CUDA-faithful guard, so no code change).  Three further extra mutants (slot-1 capacity dist, dropped counter decrement, single-round clamp) all killed; spot re-runs of 15 claimed kills confirmed; residual divergences are theoretical only (NOIDX vs lane on all-FLT_MAX/NaN inputs &mdash; unreachable with finite contact distances; the guard&lt;40 only converts a would-be CUDA hang into termination). A normal-constraint loop (recipResponse=(unit>0)?1/(unit+cfm):0; when restitutionXdt<0 the local computeCompliantContactCoefficientsTGS `a`-overload — constraintPrepShared.cuh L365-376 — overwrites velMultiplier+biasCoefficient; deltaBias = deltaV + delta0.top·raXn - delta1.top·rbXn - targetVel*elapsedTime, sep = PxMax(minPen, separation+deltaBias), biased = PxMin(-maxPenBias, biasCoefficient*sep), tVelBias = recipResponse*biased, and the NO-impulseMul force update _newForce = appliedForce + _deltaF), a PAIRED-friction loop (two rows/iter; per-row velMultiplier = (resp>PX_EPS_REAL)?p8/(resp+cfm):0 WITH cfm; error delta = raXnK·delta0.top - rbXnK·delta1.top + normalK·relMotion; combined magnitude totalImpulse=sqrt(t0²+t1²); ratio = min(maxDyn,total)/total when total>maxFrictionImpulse), and a torsional tail (torsionalFrictionScale, angular-only normalVel, PxClamp(total,-maxDyn*fs,maxDyn*fs), bottom-only imp accumulation) — the whole friction block gated on numFrictionConstr!=0 so a lone torsional (totalFrictionConstr==1) is skipped exactly as CUDA. CRITICAL: the final impulse scale is SYMMETRIC (impulse0=imp0.scale(invMass0*ref0, angDom0*ref0), impulse1=imp1.scale(invMass1*ref1, angDom1*ref1) — both bodies top*invMass, bottom*angDom), NOT the asCtPGS asymmetry (body0 top*angDom0/bottom*invMass0); pinned by the f1-f4 PGS-asymmetry-swap mutants. 3200-fixture suite (seed 188001) covering compliant/accel/normal-clamp/paired-ratio-clamp/broken/minForce/eps-guard/torsional/torsional-clamp/zero-response/ref-scaling; floats eqFZ; 54/54 mutation battery killed. This completes the arti solve cores (PGS 1D/contact 185/186, TGS 1D/contact 187/188). `npArtiCheckActive.slang` (ceacPGS + ceacTGS) ports the two light articulation active-contact predicates checkExtActiveContactBlock (solver.cuh L322-426) and checkExtActiveContactBlockTGS (solver.cuh L737-831): each mirrors the NORMAL-loop force update of its full ext contact solve (asCtPGS/asCtTGS) but returns a bool — is any contact active (|deltaF| > 1e-8) — instead of mutating velocity/impulse. Three structural facts preserved: NO ref-scaling (resp0/resp1 unscaled, no ref0/ref1 params); velocity is NOT propagated between contacts, so each contact's deltaF is an INDEPENDENT function of the shared initial velocity and the CUDA early-return is faithfully derived as the disjunction of all per-contact |deltaF|>1e-8; the arti response loads (deltaRA*/deltaRB*) are a dead memory-schedule (never consumed) and omitted. computeContactCoefficients (PGS, with impulseMul) and computeCompliantContactCoefficientsTGS (TGS a-overload) transcribed locally, as in asCtPGS/asCtTGS. This completes the PGS/TGS articulation ext solve+check family — the remaining artiSolveBlockPartition driver kernels are batch-iterate + dispatch glue. `npSlabRefCount.slang` (casCount) ports countActiveSlabs (solverBlock.cuh L84-97), the mass-splitting slab reference-count helper the (rigid and articulation) solve-block drivers use to derive ref0/ref1: for a solver body it sums __popc over num32Slabs = ceil(numSlabs/32) encoded reference-count words (strided by numSolverBodies through the global encodedReferenceCounts buffer) and returns PxMax(1u, sum). __popc -> countbits; the PxMax(1u,.) integer floor guarantees a non-zero divisor; pure unsigned-integer arithmetic (no float exception classes, so not in PXG_NOCONTRACT_SHADERS). 4000-fixture suite (seed 190001) over a shared 16384-word buffer of dense/sparse/single-bit/zero patterns, covering single- and multi-word striding (num32Slabs 1..5) and the all-zero case where the PxMax floor fires; outputs compared exactly; 10/10 mutation battery killed (the +31/32 rounding, the stride, the popcount, the accumulate, the max-floor). This is the last bit-exact-portable compute unit on the articulation solve path — the artiSolveBlockPartition driver that consumes it (velocity gather/scatter, batch dispatch to the ported solveExt*/checkExt* cores, deferred-Z impulse writeback) is addressing/dispatch glue, and resetSlabCount (the strided zero-writer companion) is trivial glue. Task #170 (gpuarticulation) opens with `npArtiPropagate.slang` (apImpulse), a port of propagateImpulseW_everythingPreLoaded (articulationDynamic.cuh L167-185): the Featherstone/Mirtich propagation of a spatial impulse from a child link to its parent through the inbound joint. For each of the (up to 3) joint dofs it forms stZ = -motionMatrix[i].innerProduct(Z), accumulates qstZ[i] += stZ, and adds isInvD[i]*stZ into the running spatial impulse, then translates the result from child to parent frame. CRITICAL: Cm::UnAlignedSpatialVector::innerProduct is the spatial motion/force DUALITY product (a.bottom.dot(b.top) + a.top.dot(b.bottom), CmSpatialVector.h L398-401), NOT the Euclidean dot (a.top.dot(b.top) + a.bottom.dot(b.bottom)) — pinned by the ipdot mutant that swaps to the dot pairing. translateSpatialVector(offset, vec) = (vec.top, vec.bottom + offset x vec.top) (DyFeatherstoneArticulation.h L828-831). 3000-fixture suite (seed 191001) covering dof counts 0..3 (dof0 = translate-only), zero and nonzero childToParent, and nonzero qstZ initial so the accumulation is observable; floats eqFZ; 10/10 mutation battery killed (the innerProduct-vs-dot trap, the stZ sign, Z-vs-temp source, qstZ accumulate, the isInvD top/bottom lanes, the translate offset x top cross, its sign, cross component order, dof clamp). `npArtiPropagateAccel.slang` (apAccel) ports propagateAccelerationW_everythingPreLoaded (articulationDynamic.cuh L206-242), the dual of apImpulse (191): it propagates an acceleration (or velocity) from a PARENT link down to a child link through the inbound joint. pDeltaV = translateSpatialVector(-parentToChild, parentAccel); a first pass forms tJointDelta[i] = QMinusSTZ[i] - IsW[i].innerProduct(pDeltaV) from the pre-accumulation pDeltaV; a second pass then accumulates pDeltaV += motionMatrix[i] * (invStISW[i] . tJointDelta) where the jDelta is the full three-lane dot of the inverse joint-space-inertia row with tJointDelta (lanes >= dofCount stay 0). Traps: the translate offset is NEGATED (-parentToChild); the innerProduct is again the spatial duality product (IsW.bottom.dot(pDeltaV.top) + IsW.top.dot(pDeltaV.bottom)), not the Euclidean dot; and the two-pass ordering is load-bearing (every tJointDelta reads the translated-but-not-yet-accumulated pDeltaV) — pinned by the nopreb mutant (which reads the raw parentAccel bottom instead of the translated one). 3000-fixture suite (seed 192001) covering dof 0..3 (dof0 = translate-only), zero/nonzero parentToChild and nonzero QMinusSTZ; floats eqFZ; 11/11 mutation battery killed (negated offset, innerProduct-vs-dot, tJointDelta sign and Q term, the jDelta 3-dot and its lane permutation, the motionMatrix top/bottom lanes, cross order, dof clamp). `npArtiPropagateAccelJV.slang` (apAccelJV) ports propagateAccelerationW_nothingpreloaded_updateJointDofVels (articulationDynamic.cuh L329-386): the same parent->child acceleration propagation as apAccel (192) but with an additional output — it writes back the updated joint dof velocities. pDeltaV = translateSpatialVector(-parentToChild, parentAccel); tJointDelta starts at mDeferredQstZ and subtracts IsW[i].innerProduct(pDeltaV); jDelta = invStIsT[i] . tJointDelta; mJointVelocities[i] = jVel[i] + jDelta; pDeltaV += motionMatrix[i]*jDelta. (In CUDA the inputs are block-data loads; modelled here as plain per-dof arrays — the loads carry no computation.) Same traps as 192 (negated translate offset, spatial-duality innerProduct, two-pass ordering) plus the joint-velocity writeback. 3000-fixture suite (seed 193001) over dof 0..3, zero/nonzero parentToChild, nonzero mDeferredQstZ and nonzero jVel seed so both the subtract and the velocity writeback observe their start values; floats eqFZ; 15/15 mutation battery killed (adds jvwrite/jvsign/jvidx pinning the jVel[i] + jDelta writeback, and tjdseed/tjdq). Adversarial review caught a real PORT-BUG here: the first cut seeded tJointDelta from ALL three mDeferredQstZ lanes, but CUDA (L341/L353) zeroes tJointDelta and assigns mDeferredQstZ only for ind<dofCount, so inactive lanes must stay 0 — otherwise the full-3-lane jDelta dot leaks spurious invStIsT.{y,z}*qstz.{y,z} terms for 1- and 2-dof joints (995/3000 fixtures diverged). Fixed forward in both shader and reference (zero-init + gated seed; inactive-lane qstz is a don't-care) and pinned by the tjdseed regression mutant. `npArtiSpatialMatVec.slang` (smMulVec) ports Dy::SpatialMatrix::operator* (const Cm::UnAlignedSpatialVector&) (DyFeatherstoneArticulationUtils.h L276-282): the spatial-inertia matrix applied to a spatial vector, used throughout the articulation dynamics (e.g. PxcFsFlushVelocity's invInertia * deferredZ). The SpatialMatrix is the block matrix [[topLeft, topRight],[bottomLeft, topLeft^T]] (the bottom-right block is topLeft TRANSPOSED, getBottomRight L246-249); applied to s=(top,bottom): top = topLeft*s.top + topRight*s.bottom, bottom = bottomLeft*s.top + topLeft.transformTranspose(s.bottom). PxMat33 is column-major: transform(v) = column0*v.x + column1*v.y + column2*v.z; transformTranspose(v) = (column0.dot(v), column1.dot(v), column2.dot(v)). The load-bearing trap is the bottom term using topLeft^T (transformTranspose), not topLeft or bottomLeft — pinned by the tt mutant (transform instead of transpose) and ttcol (transpose column-order swap); an asymmetric-topLeft majority (with a symmetric-topLeft coverage stripe) makes transpose-vs-not genuinely observable. 3000-fixture suite (seed 194001) with zero-top and zero-bottom stripes isolating the four matrix-vector products; floats eqFZ; 9/9 mutation battery killed (the transpose trap, its column order and matrix source, the three block/vector pairings, the column/component mapping, the top/bottom output). This is the SpatialMatrix primitive the larger forwardDynamic2/inverseDynamic Featherstone recursions build on. `npArtiBitfield.slang` (bitScan) ports the 64-bit ArticulationBitField scan helpers articulationLowestSetBit / articulationHighestSetBit (articulationImpulseResponse.cuh L47-57), used to iterate the articulation link bitfields: lowestSetBit(val) = val==0 ? 0 : __ffsll(val)-1 (index of the lowest set bit), highestSetBit(val) = 63 - __clzll(val) (index of the highest set bit). The 64-bit value is passed as a uint2 (lo, hi) and __ffsll/__clzll are realised per-32-bit-word via firstbitlow/firstbithigh (SPIR-V FindILsb/FindUMsb): lowest = lo!=0 ? firstbitlow(lo) : (hi!=0 ? 32+firstbitlow(hi) : 0); highest = hi!=0 ? 32+firstbithigh(hi) : firstbithigh(lo). Pure unsigned-integer arithmetic (not in PXG_NOCONTRACT_SHADERS). 4000-fixture suite (seed 195001) over single-bit, all-ones, lo-only, hi-only, the bit-31/32 word boundary and val==0 patterns; reference uses __builtin_ctzll/__builtin_clzll; outputs compared exactly; 8/8 mutation battery killed (the __ffsll-1 offset, the lo/hi split boundary +32, the val==0 guard, and the firstbitlow/firstbithigh mixups). highestSetBit(0) is UB in CUDA (__clzll(0)); both shader and reference emit firstbithigh(0)=0xFFFFFFFF and the fixture does not rely on it. propagateAccelerationWNoJVelUpdate (same header L59-113) is computationally identical to apAccel (192) modulo block-data loads and is classified as already-covered. `npArtiSpatialMatMat.slang` (smMulMat) ports Dy::SpatialMatrix::operator*(const SpatialMatrix&) (DyFeatherstoneArticulationUtils.h L328-338): the articulated-inertia composition (spatial matrix times spatial matrix). newTopLeft = topLeft*s.topLeft + topRight*s.bottomLeft; newTopRight = topLeft*s.topRight + topRight*(s.topLeft^T); newBottomLeft = bottomLeft*s.topLeft + (topLeft^T)*s.bottomLeft. PxMat33 column-major mat-mat A*B = (A.transform(B.col_i)); getTranspose swaps rows<->columns.
