PxMin(dim-1,p+1)`, center skip, the defensively-dead `idx3D >= w*h*d` guard transcribed verbatim, `PxSign = (a>=0)?1:-1` comparisons, per-axis cellSize-squared distance + PxSqrt, `delta = |value-initial|`, the `0.99f*delta > distance` guard (its 0.99 factor pinned by a DETERMINISTIC boundary-window stripe with distance/delta ~ 0.995 in [0.99, 1) -- the only ratio band where the factor is observable; found as a battery survivor and closed), `newValue = PxMin(newValue, |0.99*(distance/delta)*initial|)`, the negative-restore and the `newValue != initialValue` change flag.  The 27-value neighborhood block collapses the global sdf gathers; an id-out-of-domain stripe covers the outer guard.  Mutation battery: 15 mutants (XOR/convert forms, center pairing, both quantization scales, clamp drop, loop-bound underflow, center-skip polarity, sign compare, distance axis, guard factor, v-scale, min/max, negative-restore polarity, change flag), 15/15 caught, zero survivors ✅. `npClothTriContact.slang` ports the FINAL trivial-leaf batch: both `computeTriangleContact` variants (`FEMClothExternalSolve.cu` L155-194) -- the float2-barycentric form's `u = PxReal(1.0 - bx - by)` is an f64 SPOT (the double literal promotes the subtraction chain; transcribed as `float((double(1.0) - double(bx)) - double(by))` and pinned by an f32-demotion mutant), the weighted sum `v0*u + v1*bx + v2*by` over ALL FOUR float4 lanes, and the invMass export -- plus `bvhComputeTotalInvEdges` (`SDFConstruction.cu` L245-251, `(upper-lower) + 0.0001f` then per-component reciprocals) and `pow3`/`rangesOverlaps` (L441-448, u32 wrap-consistent cube incl. a 2^32-wrap stripe; `!(minA > maxB || minB > maxA)` with an exact touching-boundary stripe pinning the strict `>`).  Mutation battery: 8 mutants, 8/8 caught, zero survivors ✅.

**MODULE CLOSE -- gpusimulationcontroller deformables/particles block.**  With tranches 231-263 the bit-exact-portable inventory of this block is EXHAUSTED.  A very-thorough survey (softBody.cu, FEMClothExternalSolve.cu, FEMCloth.cu/FEMClothUtil.cuh, softBodyGM.cu, particlesystem.cu, SDFConstruction.cu/bvh.cuh) confirms everything remaining falls into the documented NON-PORTABLE/glue classes: (1) atomicAdd accumulation and stream-compaction cores (updateTrianglePosDelta*, reference-count queries, globalScanExclusive tails, bottom-up bvhBuildHierarchy, AtomicMax/MinVec3, block reductions); (2) solver-body/articulation reads (prepareFEMContacts, FEMCollision/PxgVelocityReader consumers, all rigid-contact prepare/solve kernels); (3) transcendentals without cross-backend rounding guarantees -- `extractRotation` (deformableUtils.cuh L46-67; its `PxQuat(w, omega)` ANGLE-AXIS constructor evaluates sinf/cosf) and therefore the dependent rotation composites computeGMTetrahedronRotations/computeSimTetrahedronRotations/computeTetrahedronRotations, plus the atan2f call sites already modeled as input seams in tranches 256/257; (4) fast-math intrinsics (__frsqrt_rn/rsqrtf/__fdividef users); (5) tex3D/surf3D SDF sampling paths (sdfCollision.cuh and the sdfMarkRequiredSdfSubgrids/sdfPopulateSdfSubgrids family -- OFF LIMITS per mandate); (6) arithmetic-free packing/DMA glue (bvhBuildLeaves/makeNode field stores, blockCopy staging, dead code such as sb_plasticDeformLaunch under if(false) and the limitLength passthrough).  (Earlier: the reference-frame setup's 2 orthoGate survivors are equivalent mutants — both in the orthoGate (AND→OR, and incNt→incN), which is an always-true defensive near-orthogonality guard in the reachable distribution: T138 selects the best-*aligned* face so |normal·refN| and |normal·incNt| are ≥1e-6 (empirically min 1.1e-6 over 153k cases, gate never false; cases near the threshold are margin-skipped), so neither change alters the always-true result; adversarial review found no divergence.  The cold-start gjk() driver (129) is now subsumed; the plus the convex/mesh/full-heightfield collision drivers and the (texture-filtered, not bit-exact) SDF sampling, remain ⬜. `npBoxClip.slang` opens the box-box collision port with its self-contained clipping geometry primitives — contains (2D point-in-polygon crossing test with the AABB early-out and the exact endpoint/epsilon handling) and intersectSegmentAABB (parametric segment-vs-AABB slab test with parallel-axis guards) — verified against C++ references over rotated quads (so the 0/2-crossing paths fire) and hit/miss/parallel segments. `npBoxIncident.slang` adds the two warp-cooperative (width-4) box-box setup primitives — getIncidentPolygon4 (broadcast `__shfl_sync` of `|column·axis|` from lanes 0/1/2 to pick box B's incident face, then each of the 4 group lanes emits one face-corner vertex transformed into box A's space, plus the group-uniform face normal) and calculateBounds4 (the width-4 `__shfl_xor_sync` butterfly AABB reduction, xor-1 include then xor-2 min/max, leaving every lane holding the group AABB), both reproduced with a 256-thread block = 64 groups-of-4 groupshared exchange and verified against per-group C++ references (all three face-axis branches exercised; bounds compared bit-exactly) ✅. `npBoxContacts.slang` ports the clipping core calculateContacts — turning box B's incident polygon (4 points, one per lane, in box A's reference-face frame) into up to 3 contacts per lane from three sources: penetrating in-face vertices ("area"), reference-face corners inside the incident polygon (the `contains` point-in-polygon test lifted onto the incident plane), and incident-polygon edges clipped against the reference-face slab (`intersectSegmentAABB`); the two `__all_sync` warp early-exits become predication (`allArea`/`done2`) so the block-wide barriers stay uniform, the per-lane pen/area flags shuffle through groupshared, and contacts are written in the CUDA's interleaved `4*c+L` slot layout — verified against a full C++ transcription (per-lane count exact, contacts to tolerance) with measure-zero predicate-boundary groups margin-skipped ✅. `npBoxSat.slang` ports the separating-axis test + feature/sign selection half of doBoxBoxGenerateContacts — the 15 OBB axes (3 face axes of A → feature 0-2, 3 of B → feature 3-5, 9 edge-edge cross axes that reject only), each a `contactDist`-inflated radius test built from `transform0.transformTranspose(transform1)` and the `abs0To1` row-magnitude matrix; the width-4 group publishes per-lane (minOverlap, feature, sign, separated) to groupshared and every lane reduces (the monotonic separation becomes a single OR, the min-overlap feature a cross-lane pick), verified against a C++ transcription of all 15 axes (overlap + feature exact, sign to tolerance; all 6 features and both overlap/separated outcomes exercised) with measure-zero separation/feature-gate boundaries margin-skipped ✅. `npBoxFeature.slang` ports the feature→newTransformV switch that consumes the SAT result: for each of the 6 features × 2 signs it builds the minimum-translation axis `mtd`, the reference-face frame `newTransformV` (rotation columns + origin) and its two in-plane half-extents `e0/e1`, then derives `transform1ToNew = newTransformV.transformTranspose(other)` and `localNormal = newTransformV.m.transformTranspose(mtd)` — exactly the inputs to getIncidentPolygon4.  This step is per-pair uniform (no warp cooperation), so it runs one thread per pair; because feature/sign are uploaded the sole `0>=sign` branch matches GPU/CPU bit-for-bit and every other output is deterministic float arithmetic — verified against a C++ transcription of all 6 cases (all outputs to tolerance, all 6 features and both signs exercised, no margin-skip needed) ✅. `npBoxReduce.slang` ports the Stage-2 tail (contact compaction/reduction): the width-4 inclusive prefix sum of the per-lane contact counts, then — if the group total ≤ 6 — a compacted global-space store at each contact's prefix offset, else the 4-point manifold reduction (deepest by penetration, furthest in the incident-face plane, and the two perpendicular extrema, each an `minIndex4` argmin/argmax with lowest-lane tie-break) transformed to global space; the group-uniform ≤6-vs->6 decision keeps all reduction barriers unconditional (only the writes predicated) so block-wide barriers stay uniform, verified against a full C++ transcription of both paths (total exact, contacts to tolerance, both paths exercised) with the argmin/argmax near-tie groups margin-skipped ✅.  **With this the box-box contact generator (doBoxBoxGenerateContacts) is ported end-to-end** — SAT/feature-select → reference-frame setup → incident-face select → clipping core → contact reduction; the GJK/EPA and convex/mesh/heightfield collision kernels remain ⬜ |
| gpuarticulation | 4 files (Featherstone forward/inverse dynamics) | 🟡 | `dmaArtiResidualBda.slang` (dmaArticulationResidual ✅, P8.66) — the first kernel of the block, and the one that establishes the `PxgArticulationCoreDesc` address map every other articulation kernel needs (`pxgSlangArticulationDesc.slang`). The dynamics themselves (`computeUnconstrained*`, `computeMassMatrix1T`, `artiPropagate*`, `artiSolveInternal*`) ⬜ |

## Host-side runtime

| Component | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Compute device abstraction (buffers, pipelines, dispatch, sync) | ✅ | `PxgSlangComputeDevice` |
| Vulkan backend | ✅ | verified on llvmpipe (CI) — any Vulkan ≥ 1.2 device |
| D3D12 backend | 🟡 | complete implementation (WARP + hardware adapters, root constants, structured UAVs); compiles/executes only on Windows — per-kernel HLSL cross-compilation is ctest-enforced everywhere |
| Offline shader build (slangc → SPIR-V/DXIL) | ✅ | CMake rules in `tests/CMakeLists.txt` |
| Replacement of `PxgCudaMemoryAllocator`/`PxgHeapMemAllocator` pools | ⬜ | tests use direct buffer allocation |
| Integration into `PxgPhysXGpu` module loading (`PhysXGpu` DLL) | ⬜ | requires replacing the CUDA-context plumbing in `physxgpu/` |

All ported kernels are covered by `tests/` (237 GPU test cases, run per commit
via ctest on a Vulkan device — llvmpipe in CI):

Distinct dirty→persistent indices avoid write races; non-vacuity confirmed by mutation (scatter dest swap, a selective field leak, and a clear-prevSize break each fail) |

| aggregate sort projections | C++ reference building the same sorted structure | sortAndUpdateAggregateProjections is verified over several aggregates (some invalid) each with member boxes given globally-distinct even mins / odd maxes: the built min/max projections + start/end handles, the sorted order, the per-box sapBox1D start/end positions, the start-projection bitmask and the local bound-index list are all compared exactly against a C++ reference (the CUDA warp radix sort is replaced by an output-identical stable rank sort — provably equal to a stable value sort with original-index tie-break); non-vacuity confirmed by mutation (the min/max projection offset and the sapBox start/end position each fail) |
| clampMaxValue(s) | scalar clamp | in-place counter clamping |
| skinning | C++ transcription of `utility.cu` | area-weighted vertex normals (atomic accumulation), Phong cloth interpolation incl. out-of-triangle uv projection and thickness clamping, barycentric softbody interpolation, buffer interleaving, strided-view addressing |

Floating-point comparisons use relative tolerance 1e-4…1e-5 (GPU `sin/cos/
sqrt` and FMA contraction differ legitimately from the CPU); all integer,
flag and index outputs are compared exactly.
